- **notes-to-llms-txt**: Turn disorganized notes — a scratch file, a run of meeting notes, a mixed-topic dump with todos and facts and half-finished thoughts tangled together — into a well-formed llms.txt family (index, full, small, facts). - **notes-to-obsidian**: 把散落在得到大脑、腾讯ima、飞书文档/妙记、Plaud等录音卡、钉钉闪记、通义听悟、讯飞、Notion、印象笔记里的笔记和录音逐字稿汇总到本地，用 AI 按企业提效、自媒体两条线分类，生成带看板的 Obsidian 个人知识库，数据全在自己电脑上；装好后带小白上手，能用知识库整理客户需求、会议待办、SOP，或按固定框架写短视频口播逐字稿。用户说“把笔记做成知识库”“把录音卡/妙记/ima/得到的内容导进 Obsidian”“搭个人知识库”“第二大脑”“更新知识库”“用知识库写口播”“用知识库提效”时使用。不用于在原平台新建、修改或删除笔记。 ([Ericxiang2025/notes-to-obsidian](https://github.com/Ericxiang2025/notes-to-obsidian/tree/HEAD/notes-to-obsidian) · ★4)
- **notes-to-obsidian-vault**: 把按页/按日期组织的 Markdown 笔记（题库、错题本、课堂笔记、词条本等）批量迁移成 Obsidian 独立库：一条一篇原子笔记、双链挂方法、标签挂知识点、frontmatter 存档案，并保留原页做对照。适用于「把这些笔记搬到 Obsidian」「拆题/一条一篇」「建个数学库/错题库」「笔记转双链」这类需求，也适用于已经迁过一次、需要重建或增量校验的场景。内置探查、建库、校验三个脚本，以及一套已验证的领域配置样例。 ([play1216/notes-to-obsidian-vault](https://github.com/play1216/notes-to-obsidian-vault/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **notes-to-outline**: 把用户提供的笔记整理成有来源的文章大纲。 ([jiangzl1986-gif/skill-workbench](https://github.com/jiangzl1986-gif/skill-workbench/tree/HEAD/examples/notes-to-outline) · ★1)
- **notes-to-outline-synthesis**: "从笔记到主题大纲"的综合转化法 — 当你读完一堆文献、做好电子表格笔记，却打开空白文档完全不知道从哪开始写时触发。 核心动作：确立论点 → 创建主题大纲（论证路线图）→ 重组笔记映射到大纲节点 → 用分析细节充实大纲 → 开始撰写初稿。 触发信号："读完了文献不知道怎么开始写""笔记散在各处写不出来""综述没论点没结构""大纲怎么写""怎么把笔记变成文章"。 不适用于：还没完成文献检索和深度分析（应先调用文献深度分析 skill）；已有清晰大纲只需润色的情况。 ([Rodneyli/paper-writing-skills](https://github.com/Rodneyli/paper-writing-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/12-%E7%AC%94%E8%AE%B0%E5%88%B0%E4%B8%BB%E9%A2%98%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2) · ★3)

- **notes-to-piano**: Render a scanned music score into realistic PIANO audio (WAV/MP3). Takes MusicXML/.mxl/.mid (e.g. the output of the omr-notes skill) and deterministically synthesizes it with FluidSynth + a CC-BY grand-piano soundfont — fully offline, no cloud. Optional dynamics humanization, tempo override, and score PDF. Use after omr-notes, or whenever the user wants to HEAR a MusicXML/MIDI score as piano. ([Neanderthal/omr-notes](https://github.com/Neanderthal/omr-notes/tree/HEAD/skills/notes-to-piano) · ★2)
- **notes-to-prose-paragraph**: Turns bulleted notes or loose sentences into one to three prose paragraphs, numbers every bullet, and returns a coverage table that maps each bullet to its sentence without adding any fact, cause or conclusion. Use when notes read as fragments and need paragraph form, such as a launch summary, meeting recap or project update drafted from bullets. Do not use for writing prose from a topic with no notes, summarizing a long document, or editing a paragraph that is already written. ([kobilyricsweb/skillalmanac-skills](https://github.com/kobilyricsweb/skillalmanac-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/notes-to-prose-paragraph))
- **notes-to-recall-cards**: Turn pasted study notes into a small deck of atomic recall flashcards plus a spaced review schedule that ends before the exam - one testable fact per card, each card traceable to a line of the notes, no card that only restates its own answer, trivia skipped and listed, the deck capped and prioritised, and every card checked against the notes. Use when someone wants flashcards or a review plan from their own notes. Not for inventing facts the notes lack, replacing the course material, judging a learner or generating large decks. ([modootoday/marketplace](https://github.com/modootoday/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/education/skills/notes-to-recall-cards))

- **notes_attach_image**: Attach an image file to a Notes note. Notes' AppleScript dictionary does NOT support `make new attachment` for image files, so this skill uses the clipboard-paste path: 1. Read the image into the system pasteboard via `osascript`'s `(read POSIX file "X" as JPEG picture)` coercion (works for JPEG / PNG / TIFF — checks file extension). 2. Activate Notes, select the target note, focus the body. 3. Move cursor to end of body, then Cmd+V — Notes pastes a pasteboard image as an attachment in the note. This is more robust than the Edit-menu "Attach File" UI flow, which opens a file picker that's bri… ([LocalKinAI/kinclaw](https://github.com/LocalKinAI/kinclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/notes_attach_image) · ★9)
- **notes_checklist**: Convert a note's body into a Notes-native checklist (HTML `&lt;ul class="gtl-todo-list"&gt;` markup), with optional auto-checking of specific item indices. Agents routinely fail at this by typing markdown `- [ ]` text — Notes does NOT recognize markdown checklist syntax; it stores literal "- [ ]" text. The native checklist requires Cmd+Shift+L on selected text or Format menu → Checklist. This skill: activates Notes, focuses the note body, selects all, Cmd+Shift+L to convert. If `check_indices` is given, navigates to each line and presses Cmd+Shift+U to toggle the checkbox. ([LocalKinAI/kinclaw](https://github.com/LocalKinAI/kinclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/notes_checklist) · ★9)
- **notes_export_pdf**: Export a Notes note to a PDF file at an absolute path. Notes doesn't expose Export-as-PDF via AppleScript dictionary; this skill drives the File menu + Save sheet via UI scripting. The Save sheet is the standard NSSavePanel — we type the filename into the focused field, use Cmd+Shift+G to set the destination directory, then press Return to commit. The skill does NOT use the Print → Save as PDF route (3+ extra dialog clicks); it uses Notes' direct File → Export as PDF... menu. After the save, the skill polls for the file's existence to confirm completion before returning. ([LocalKinAI/kinclaw](https://github.com/LocalKinAI/kinclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/notes_export_pdf) · ★9)
- **notes_format**: Apply a Notes-app text format (bold / italic / underline / title / heading / subheading / body) to text in a note. Handles selection + format keystroke + verification. The #1 mistake agents make: pressing Cmd+B without selecting text first. Notes' format shortcuts only act on the current selection; no selection = no-op. This skill always selects first. ([LocalKinAI/kinclaw](https://github.com/LocalKinAI/kinclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/notes_format) · ★9)

- **notes_move_to_folder**: Move a note (by exact title match) into a Notes folder (by exact folder name). Pure AppleScript — no UI scripting, no flakiness. The folder must already exist; create it with osascript or via the Notes UI before calling this skill. Why a skill (not raw shell osascript): models routinely emit the wrong AppleScript syntax for `move`, hit "Can't make ... into type specifier" errors, or move into the wrong account's folder. This skill encapsulates the canonical pattern + cross-account search. ([LocalKinAI/kinclaw](https://github.com/LocalKinAI/kinclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/notes_move_to_folder) · ★9)
- **notes_pin**: Pin or unpin a Notes note (by exact title match). Uses UI menu navigation because macOS 14+ removed the AppleScript `pinned` property — direct `set pinned of note to true` errors with "Can't make pinned of note ... into type specifier". This skill activates Notes, selects the target note, then clicks the appropriate File-menu item ("Pin Note" or "Unpin Note"). The menu item label automatically toggles based on current state, so the skill checks which one exists and clicks the right one for the requested target state. ([LocalKinAI/kinclaw](https://github.com/LocalKinAI/kinclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/notes_pin) · ★9)
